# R27 changed macOS/layout-gated fixture inventory

Source `a6915a368` vs r26 `188b3488c` (includes the main merges of #919, #922, #923, #925 and #926). **1833** new/changed fixture names below.
Direct rows contain a layout/macOS check in the test body; shared-file/helper
rows are a conservative superset because helpers or native branches can gate
them. No claim that the entire superset is Mac-only. This prevents omitting a
changed fixture behind an indirect gate. Most rows are r27's `terminal_off_on_*` twins: each restores main's exact test
and moves the #899 version to an ON twin, so the twin is new while main's
name is unchanged from main. Ignored readiness twins are listed too.
No layout/size pin changed. Branch Mac remains required; none dispatched here.
